### 中文

警戒的船员保持待命，时刻准备探测敌方舰队，绝不允许对方发动哪怕是无用而怯懦的骚扰。

### 中文

有一位正直的指挥官坐镇中枢，舰队整装待发，将对任何侵犯我国主权的行径发动报复打击。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:137`

## leader_trait_bulwark_sentinel_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_bulwark_sentinel_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

英勇的船员即使面对敌人也能维持战线，随时准备把那些胆敢进攻我们宇域的蠢货揍回老家！

## resource_subsidies_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresource_subsidies_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该条款指示附属国每月应当上缴给宗主国的资源数量。这个数字是附属国当前每月生产量的一个百分比。\n\n若此值为负数，意味着宗主国应当给予附属国资源而非反之，数额取决于附属国当前每月的生产量的一定比例。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:167`

### English

This Term dictates the amount of resources the Subject is obligated to pay to the Overlord each month. The number is a percentage of the Subject's current monthly production.\n\nA negative value means the Overlord is obligated to pay the Subject rather than the other way around, but it is still based on a percentage of the Subject's current monthly production.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:180`

## AGREEMENT_RESOURCE_TAX

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AAGREEMENT_RESOURCE_TAX&lang=en)

### 中文

附属国将会上缴自身每月$RESOURCE|Y$产出的$VALUE|Y$给宗主国。\n\n$AGREEMENT_RESOURCE_BLOCKS_TRADE$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:168`

### English

The Subject will pay $VALUE|Y$ tax of their monthly $RESOURCE|Y$ production to the Overlord.\n\n$AGREEMENT_RESOURCE_BLOCKS_TRADE$

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:181`

## AGREEMENT_RESOURCE_SUBSIDIES

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AAGREEMENT_RESOURCE_SUBSIDIES&lang=en)

### 中文

宗主国会提供数额相当于附属国每月$RESOURCE|Y$产出$VALUE|Y$的津贴给附属国。\n\n$AGREEMENT_RESOURCE_BLOCKS_TRADE$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:169`

### English

The Overlord will provide the Subject with a subsidy matching $VALUE|Y$ of their monthly $RESOURCE|Y$ production.\n\n$AGREEMENT_RESOURCE_BLOCKS_TRADE$

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:182`

### 中文

该条款指示附属国被授予多少外交自由，以及他们是否允许不参考宗主国意见自由投票。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:227`

### English

### 中文

### English

## subject_can_do_diplomacy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asubject_can_do_diplomacy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

附属国可以自由地与大多数其他帝国展开外交，并拥有独立投票权。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:229`

### English

The Subject is free to engage in most diplomacy with other Empires and vote independently.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:249`

## subject_can_do_diplomacy_but_not_vote

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asubject_can_do_diplomacy_but_not_vote&lang=en)

### 中文

投票受限

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:230`

### English

Restricted Voting

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:250`

## subject_can_do_diplomacy_but_not_vote_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asubject_can_do_diplomacy_but_not_vote_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

附属国可以自由地与大多数其他帝国展开外交，但是在星海共同体中必须跟随其宗主国投票。\n\n这会强制附属国加入宗主国所归属的§Y联邦§!。\n\n如果宗主国是§Y星海共同体§!的一员，那么附属国也会强制加入。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:231`

### English

The Subject is free to engage in most diplomacy, but must follow their Overlord's votes in Federations and the Galactic Community.\n\nThis will force the Subject to join any §YFederation§! that the Overlord is a member of.\n\nIf the Overlord is a member of the §YGalactic Community§!, the Subject will be forced to join it.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:251`

## subject_can_not_do_diplomacy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asubject_can_not_do_diplomacy&lang=en)

### 中文

限制外交

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:232`

### English

Limited Diplomacy

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:252`

## subject_can_not_do_diplomacy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asubject_can_not_do_diplomacy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

附属国既不能自由地与其他帝国展开外交，也没有独立投票权。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:233`

### English

The Subject may not engage in diplomacy with other Empires, nor vote independently.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:253`

## holding_garrison_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_garrison_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一支负责监管当地执法者的占领军，偶尔在极度严峻的情况下还能得到军队的增援。

## holding_garrison_armies_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_garrison_armies_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个行星上驻扎的每一支来自宗主国的陆军都给予额外的§G-10%§![Root.CrimeOrDeviancy]、§G+0.5§!$r_loyalty$以及§R-5%§!幸福度，最大可达到§G-30%§![Root.CrimeOrDeviancy]，以及额外的§G+1.5§!$r_loyalty$和§R-15%§!幸福度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:487`

### English

Every Overlord army on this planet gives an additional §G-10%§! [Root.CrimeOrDeviancy], §G+0.5§! $r_loyalty$ and §R-5%§! Happiness, to a maximum of §G-30%§! [Root.CrimeOrDeviancy], (an additional) §G+1.5§! $r_loyalty$ and §R-15%§! Happiness.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:530`

## holding_material_requisitorium_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_material_requisitorium_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个监管当地矿物产出、提取和纯化的部门。他们总能购得最好的土地并为他们的赞助人获得利益。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:502`

### English

A ministry overseeing local mineral production, extraction, and purification. They often secure the best land purchases and reap the benefits for their patron.

## holding_produce_requisitorium_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_produce_requisitorium_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个监管当地产出的部门。除了征收一些微不足道的税收以外，一些物产也很容易在弯弯绕绕的官僚手续当中不翼而飞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:504`

A ministry overseeing local produce. In addition to placing a small tax on this world, some products have a tendency to disappear into their convoluted, bureaucratic process.

## holding_splinter_hive_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_splinter_hive_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

用于为蜂巢生产新工蜂的一大团生物质。尽管它相对独立于蜂巢主体，但仍通过神经网络与之相连。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:513`

### English

A large biomass producing new workers for the hive. Though it stands apart from the original hive, it is still connected to the neural network.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:556`

## holding_distributed_processing

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_distributed_processing&lang=en)

分布式处理器

### English

Distributed Processing

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:557`

## holding_distributed_processing_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_distributed_processing_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一座遮蔽着整个区域的冰冷的设施。在此处的工作人员对他们的详细职责三缄其口，但从没有人能在进入过以后全身而退。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:515`

### English

A cold structure eclipsing the local region. Those who work here refuse to elaborate on what they do, but no-one leaves the experience entirely unscathed.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:558`

## holding_offspring_nest

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_offspring_nest&lang=en)

### 中文

子嗣巢穴

### English

Offspring Nest

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:559`

## holding_offspring_nest_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_offspring_nest_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在这座巢穴中，一个外来的蜂巢思维派出一位独特的代表作为其神经网络中央节点。这使其可以维持对殖民地的控制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:517`

### English

At this nest, a foreign hivemind has a unique representative acting as a centralized node for its neural network. This has enabled its continued control over the colony.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:560`

## holding_offworld_foundry

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_offworld_foundry&lang=en)

### 中文

外域铸造厂

### English

Offworld Foundry

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:561`

## holding_offworld_foundry_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_offworld_foundry_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

设立在外星帝国内的大型铸造厂，将当地的资源集中起来，生产出高质量的合金。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:519`

### English

Large foundries owned by a foreign empire, siphoning off local resources for the creation of high quality alloys.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:562`

Vigil Command

## holding_overlord_vigil_command_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_overlord_vigil_command_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一处指挥中心，专门针对——专门负责——计算提升防御措施的可行手段。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:521`

A command center specialized in - and delegated to - calculating the best possible means of increasing defensive measures.

## holding_parasitic_algorithms

科研部

### English

Ministry of Science

## holding_parasitic_algorithms_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_parasitic_algorithms_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个负责监督当地科学与研究进展的部门，将为其雇主带回最有价值的突破和发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:523`

### English

A ministry overseeing local research and science, bringing the most noteworthy breakthroughs and discoveries back to their benefactor.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:566`

## holding_parasitic_algorithms_effect_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_parasitic_algorithms_effect_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.ResearchProducerWithIcon]将附属国的[Root.SubjectResearcherUpkeep]转化为宗主国的$r_research$。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:524`

### English

[Root.ResearchProducerWithIcon] turn Subject [Root.SubjectResearcherUpkeep] into $r_research$ for the Overlord.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:567`

## holding_noble_vacation_homes

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_noble_vacation_homes&lang=en)

### 中文

贵族行宫

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:525`

### English

Noble Chateaus

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:569`

## holding_noble_vacation_homes_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_noble_vacation_homes_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

身在异乡亦有家，此处便是宗主国精英阶层们的放浪形骸之地；只要在这里得到充分的释放，就不会在家里惹乱子。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:526`

### English

A home away from home, this is the perfect place for the Overlord's ruling elite to let off steam, so that they don't cause incidents back home.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:570`

## holding_noble_vacation_homes_effect

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_noble_vacation_homes_effect&lang=en)

### 中文

每座$holding_noble_vacation_homes$给予宗主国的首都§G+2§!$r_stability$。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:527`

### English

Each $holding_noble_vacation_homes$ grants §G+2§! $r_stability$ in the Overlord's capital.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:571`

## holding_dread_outpost

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_dread_outpost&lang=en)

### 中文

恐惧前哨

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:528`

### English

Dread Outpost

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:573`

## holding_dread_outpost_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_dread_outpost_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

恐惧前哨是一座神秘的建筑，它可以产生邪恶的亡者大军，同时将阴暗的数据发送回这个行星的宗主国。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:529`

### English

The Dread Outpost is an arcane structure that produces unholy reanimated armies while sending back insidious data to this planet's Overlord.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:574`

## holding_communal_housing_outreach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_communal_housing_outreach&lang=en)

### 中文

公共住房计划推广

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:530`

### English

Communal Housing Outreach

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:576`

## holding_communal_housing_outreach_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_communal_housing_outreach_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

人人都拥有居住权，因此宗主将他们的公共住房计划推广到了这颗星球上。这项计划可以充当一个向当地住民传播平权主义价值观的绝佳论坛。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:531`

### English

Everyone is entitled to a home, which is why the Overlord has extended their Communal Housing Project to this planet. This serves as a great forum for teaching the inhabitants the value of egalitarianism.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:577`

## holding_idyllic_bloom

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_idyllic_bloom&lang=en)

### 中文

盖亚播种器前哨

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:532`

### English

Gaia Seeder Outpost

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:579`

## holding_idyllic_bloom_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_idyllic_bloom_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

宗主所掌握的超先进环境改造技术凝聚于此建筑中。这座建筑在这颗行星上落成之后，它会花费十年的时间来完成行星改造工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:533`

### English

Hyper-advanced terraforming techniques mastered by the Overlord are housed in this structure. After being introduced to the planet, it will take a decade to finish its world-changing work.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:580`

## holding_idyllic_bloom_effect_timer

将会在§Y[Planet.idyllic_bloom_holding_timer]§!完成环境改造。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:534`

### English

Will finish terraforming on §Y[Planet.idyllic_bloom_holding_timer]§!.

## holding_reemployment_center

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_reemployment_center&lang=en)

### 中文

再就业中心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:536`

### English

Reemployment Center

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:584`

## holding_reemployment_center_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_reemployment_center_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不要浪费任何资源，包括附属国的尸体。利用再就业中心，宗主可以确保附属国的行星上永远都有充足的雇员，虽然其中工作的质量可能参差不齐。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:537`

### English

Never leave a resource unexploited, even if that resource is the Subject's dead. With the Reemployment Center, the Overlord can ensure that the Subject's planets are always filled with employees even though the quality of work might vary.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:585`

## holding_reemployment_center_effect

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_reemployment_center_effect&lang=en)

### 中文

\n§Y100§!£pop£§Y僵尸人口§!将在§Y[Planet.reemployment_center_timer]§!复苏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:538`

### English

\n§Y100§! new £pop£ §YZombie Pops§! will be reanimated on §Y[Planet.reemployment_center_timer]§!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:586`

## holding_recruitment_office

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_recruitment_office&lang=en)

### 中文

招募办公室

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:539`

### English

Recruitment Office

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:588`

## holding_recruitment_office_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_recruitment_office_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

人人都有义务尽忠职守，因此宗主向我们提供军事训练，并以观摩宗主舰队为奖赏。公民权来自于服役。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:540`

### English

Everyone must do their part. That is why the Overlord is offering military training in exchange for a tour in their fleet. Service guarantees citizenship.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:589`

## MESSAGE_OVERLORD_RECRUIT_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMESSAGE_OVERLORD_RECRUIT_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

外派任务完成

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:541`

### English

Tour of Duty Completed

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:590`

## MESSAGE_OVERLORD_RECRUIT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMESSAGE_OVERLORD_RECRUIT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

加入我们的§Y[moving_pop.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!在完成他们的外派任务后决定留在$PLANET|Y$。我们的附属国对此感到不满，但他们无权管辖我们的公民。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:542`

### English

After finishing their tour of duty, the §Y[moving_pop.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§! joining us have decided to stay on $PLANET|Y$. Our subject is not pleased, but they cannot decide over our citizens.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:591`

## holding_park_ranger_lodge

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_park_ranger_lodge&lang=en)

### 中文

护林员小屋

### English

Ranger Lodge

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:593`

## holding_park_ranger_lodge_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_park_ranger_lodge_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

宗主喜欢与自然和谐共处，并希望与他们的附庸分享这一乐趣。为了维系这种乐趣，这里始终至少保留着部分可供享受的自然环境。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:544`

### English

The Overlord has an interest in co-existing with nature and wishes to spread this joy to its Vassal. To ensure that there always is at least some nature left to enjoy.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:594`

### English

$TRIGGER_FAIL$§RNo untouched nature remains on this planet for us to protect.§!

## holding_tree_of_life_sapling

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_tree_of_life_sapling&lang=en)

### 中文

生命之树苗

### English

Tree of Life Sprout

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:599`

## holding_tree_of_life_sapling_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_tree_of_life_sapling_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位宗主同他们的生命之树已经共存许久，如今已经无法想象没有它的生活。现在，他们想要让自己的附庸们也享受同等的机遇。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:549`

### English

The Overlord has existed with their Tree of Life for so long that they can no longer imagine life without it. Now, they want to share the same opportunity with their vassals.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:600`

## no_tree_of_life_sapling_wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ano_tree_of_life_sapling_wilderness&lang=en)

$TRIGGER_FAIL$§R荒野的环境对外星幼苗而言危机四伏。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:550`

### English

$TRIGGER_FAIL$§RThe Wilderness' environment is hostile to alien saplings.§!

## holding_franchise_headquarters_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_franchise_headquarters_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些办公室利用企业管理手段，通过行星范围的强制训练和团队建设活动，保证子公司的利益同其宗主一致。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:552`

### English

These offices allow Corporate Management to realign the Subsidiary's interests with that of their Overlord through mandatory planet-wide training and team-building exercises.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:604`

## holding_experimental_crater_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_experimental_crater_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一处用于测试小行星殖民船的专门区域。或许这不是测试这种舰船最方便的方式，但将这一过程外包肯定可以节省宗主的清理成本。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:558`

### English

A designated area for testing new asteroidal colony ships. It might not be the most convenient way to test the ships, but outsourcing the process definitely saves on the clean-up costs for the Overlord.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:611`

## holding_experimental_crater_effect

### English

§YCaution:§!\n     Incoming colony "ships" may §REXPLODE§!!

### 中文

一颗来自我们宗主国的殖民小行星撞击了$PLANET|Y$。需要几个月对这颗行星进行修复。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:561`

### English

A Colony Asteroid from our Overlord has crashed into $PLANET|Y$. It will take months to repair the planet.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:614`

## holding_organic_sanctuary

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_organic_sanctuary&lang=en)

### English

Organic Haven

## holding_organic_sanctuary_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_organic_sanctuary_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管和宗主星球上的设施相比，这颗行星上的疗养设施在规模上要有限得多，然而居住其中的活体陈设仍然享受到了比过去更高的生活标准。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:563`

### English

Although the care facilities on this planet are more limited in scope than those found on the Overlord's worlds, the bio-trophies inside still enjoy a much more comfortable standard of living than they are used to.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:617`

对生死轮回的大研究不应仅仅局限于宗主国；附庸也必须探索生死之间的转变。

The great study of the cycle should not be limited to the Overlord; the turning of life and death must be explored through the Vassal as well.

一大团生物质，不断地活动、悸动，缓慢地为蜂巢生产新的劳工子个体。

## d_buried_lithoids_overlord

$d_buried_lithoids$

## d_buried_lithoids_overlord_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_buried_lithoids_overlord_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们宗主国的最新一批“测试飞行员”班组在成功地撞击着陆于[Root.GetName]之后，在那里进入休眠状态。如果将其发掘出土，或许我们可以劝说他们加入我方。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:575`

### English

The crew of our Overlord's latest "test-flight" is lying dormant after their successful crash landing into [Root.GetName]. If unearthed, they might be persuaded to join us.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:630`

## AGREEMENT_CAN_NOT_AFFORD_PROPOSE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AAGREEMENT_CAN_NOT_AFFORD_PROPOSE&lang=en)

## job_overlord_propagandist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_overlord_propagandist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为当地政府部门工作的宣传家是在该地区进行渗透和缓和局势的专家。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:688`

### English

Propagandists working for the local ministry are experts at infiltrating and defusing situations among the locals.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:752`

## job_overlord_propagandist_drone_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_overlord_propagandist_drone_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于外交目的配备的子个体，十分擅长扰乱叛乱势力的思想和理念。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:689`

### English

Diplomatically equipped drones who are experts at defusing rebellious thoughts and ideas.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:753`

## job_overlord_academic_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_overlord_academic_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

学者与来自许多帝国的专家们共同工作，开展特定的实验和研究以获取共同利益。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:699`

### English

Academics who work with specialists from several empires, conducting particular experiments and research that can benefit them all.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:764`

## job_overlord_academic_drone_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_overlord_academic_drone_desc&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:700`

### English

Specifically equipped drones who utilize resources from several empires to conduct unique experiments and research.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:765`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:710`

一个分配子个体十分擅长于重新分配外国商品，使其同胞始终维持最大的运作效率。

## job_overlord_breeder_drone_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_overlord_breeder_drone_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对新生的子个体进行养育是一项很重要的官能，确保年轻的个体能够正确、高速、有效地成长起来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:733`

### English

Feeding the newborn drones is an important part of ensuring the young spawn can grow correctly, quickly, and effectively.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:801`

## job_overlord_breeder_effect_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_overlord_breeder_effect_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

£job_overlord_breeder£ $job_overlord_breeder_plural$将附属国的[Root.OverlordConsumes]转化为宗主国首都的§G2§!£pop£§Y人口组装§!。\n在行星上生成一个§Y$d_spawning_complex_blocker$§!障碍，占据一个区划槽。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:734`

### English

£job_overlord_breeder£ $job_overlord_breeder_plural$ turn Subject [Root.OverlordConsumes] into §G2§! £pop£ §YPop Assembly§! for Overlord capital.\nAn §Y$d_spawning_complex_blocker$§! blocker will spawn on the planet, occupying a district slot.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_mind_thrall_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

The thralls come together in large groups to temporarily serve the machine network's need, renting out their minds for processing needs.

## job_overlord_offspring_drone_feeder_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_overlord_offspring_drone_feeder_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

保护和照料子嗣是一件饱受赞誉的光荣职责，尽管有时候他们的实际职责只是收集用来喂养它们的食物罢了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:754`

### English

It is a prestigious responsibility to keep the Offspring safe and comfortable, even if the role often involves merely gathering food for it.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:824`

## job_overlord_offspring_drone_feeder_drone_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_overlord_offspring_drone_feeder_drone_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

养育子嗣是一项至关重要的工作，行星的稳定与否仰仗于此。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:755`

### English

Feeding the Offspring is a vital job to ensure stability on the planet.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:825`

## job_overlord_ranger_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_overlord_ranger_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

护林员的任务是确保大自然永远以某种形式存在于这个行星之上，因此要关注任何可能威胁到这片宗主国设立的自然保护区的因素。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:829`

### English

Tasked with ensuring that nature is always present in some form on the planet, the Ranger is on the lookout for anything that could endanger the area sectioned off by the Overlord.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:905`

## job_overlord_arborist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_overlord_arborist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在这个星球上，宗主国种下了一棵取自他们世界树的嫩枝。我们的职责就是确保它永远不会枯萎，否则我们必将面临宗主国的怒火。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:840`

### English

On this world, the Overlord planted a sapling from their world tree. It is our responsibility to ensure it never withers, lest we face their wrath.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:917`

## job_overlord_bio_trophy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_overlord_bio_trophy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的宗主终日担心我们有所匮乏或者受到伤害，因此让一小群幸运儿享受他们称之为“强制给养”的非凡待遇。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:850`

### English

Worried that we might lack for anything or even be hurt, our Overlord has provided a lucky few with the opportunity to experience what they call "mandatory pampering."

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:928`

## job_overlord_bio_trophy_drone_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_overlord_bio_trophy_drone_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的宗主国指示这些子个体前往这座建筑进行“休闲”与“享乐”。宗主国或许没有完全理解一个子个体的天性，但至少这番体验是新鲜的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:854`

### English

The drones our Overlord directed to this structure are told to "relax" and "enjoy themselves." The Overlord may not fully understand the nature of a drone, but at least the sensations are novel.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:932`

## job_overlord_bio_trophy_effect_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_overlord_bio_trophy_effect_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

£job_bio_trophy£$job_overlord_bio_trophy_plural$为附属国提供一个£happiness£§Y幸福度§!提升并不花费维护费，同时为宗主国和附属国提供£unity£§Y凝聚力§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:855`

### English

£job_bio_trophy£ $job_overlord_bio_trophy_plural$ provide a £happiness£ §YHappiness§! increase and cost no upkeep for the Subject while providing the Overlord and the Subject with £unity£ §YUnity§!.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:933`

## job_overlord_mortal_initiate_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_overlord_mortal_initiate_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们宗主国的风俗习惯或许看上去奇怪而神秘，但是接受他们是我们的历史必然。只有那些心甘情愿的人会被要求作出终极的祭献。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:861`

### English

The customs of our Overlord may seem strange and mysterious, but our way forward is by accepting them. Only those who are willing will be asked to make the ultimate sacrifice.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:940`

## job_overlord_mortal_initiate_drone

### English

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:943`

## job_overlord_mortal_initiate_drone_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_overlord_mortal_initiate_drone_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们宗主国的风俗习惯或许看上去奇怪而神秘，但我们是他们的一部分。我们子个体的一小部分被指派去加入宗主国的“生命的研究”，这项研究最终会以它们的自愿祭献收场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:865`

### English

The customs of our Overlord may seem strange and mysterious, yet we are to be a part of them. A few of our drones have been designated to join what the Overlord calls 'a study of life', one which concludes with their willing sacrifice.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:944`

## job_overlord_mortal_initiate_effect_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_overlord_mortal_initiate_effect_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[mortal_initiate.GetIcon]$job_overlord_mortal_initiate_plural$为附属国产出£unity£§Y凝聚力§!，£amenities£§Y$amenity$§!以及£society£§Y研究点§!。$job_overlord_mortal_initiate$可以被用于§Y祭献§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:866`

### English

[mortal_initiate.GetIcon] $job_overlord_mortal_initiate_plural$ produce £unity£ §YUnity§!, £amenities£ §Y$amenity$§! and £society£ §YResearch§! for the Subject. $job_overlord_mortal_initiate$ can be used for §YSacrifices§!.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:945`

## job_overlord_manager_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_overlord_manager_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

中层管理人员能够优化当地白领工人的行为，使其同宗主国的长期企业规划产生协同作用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:872`

### English

Middle managers that optimize the behavior of the local white-collar workers to synergize with the Overlord's long-term corporate trajectory.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:952`

## job_overlord_manager_effect_desc

### 中文

古怪的传感器读数

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:975`

### English

Abnormal Sensor Readings

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1066`

## specialist.900.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspecialist.900.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.GetName]上获取了一些奇怪的读数，很有可能表明其上有生命存在。由于这些信号较为微弱，而且在我们试图与之联系时毫无反应，所以如果想弄清真相，我们需要派出一支研究团队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:976`

### English

We have picked up some curious readings from [From.GetName], possibly indicating the presence of life. Given the weakness of these signals and the lack of response as we attempt to contact these lifeforms, we must send a research team if we are to figure out what might have happened.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1067`

## specialist.900.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspecialist.900.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们捕捉到了一些来自[From.GetName]的读数，这可能意味着生命的存在。我们应该派遣一些[root.GetIndividualNamePlural]，搞清楚是什么在发射信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:977`

### English

We have picked up some readings from [From.GetName], possibly indicating the presence of life. We should send some [root.GetIndividualNamePlural] to look into whatever might be transmitting a signal.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1068`

## specialist.915.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspecialist.915.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们位于[From.GetName]的[root.GetIndividualNamePlural]最近在追踪古怪读数这一任务上取得了显著的进展。[Root.SensorReadings]\n\n[Root.SensorReadingsPhysics]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:987`

### English

Our [root.GetIndividualNamePlural] at [From.GetName] have recently made great progress in tracking down the strange readings. [Root.SensorReadings]\n\n[Root.SensorReadingsPhysics]

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1081`

## specialist.920.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspecialist.920.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### English

There's good news from [From.GetName] after our [root.GetIndividualNamePlural]' latest findings.

## SCHOLARIUM_PLANETARY_SENSORS

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASCHOLARIUM_PLANETARY_SENSORS&lang=en)

### 中文

学邦传感器反常

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:991`

### English

Scholarium Sensor Abnormality

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1087`

## SCHOLARIUM_PLANETARY_SENSORS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASCHOLARIUM_PLANETARY_SENSORS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.GetName]上获得了一个不寻常的信号，似乎是在尝试与我们联系。然而我们还不能与这个信号源进行沟通，如果想对其了解更多，我们需要派遣一艘舰船进行仔细检查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:992`

### English

We have picked up on uncommon signals coming from [From.GetName], seemingly trying to initiate contact with us. We have been unable to communicate with whatever or whoever it is, and will have to send a ship to inspect it more closely if we want to learn more about what it might be.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1088`

## SensorReadings1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadings1&lang=en)

### 中文

他们在目标地点遇到了一艘坠毁的舰船残骸，仍然在不时地向外发送微弱的信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:995`

### English

They came across the debris of a ship which had crashed at the location, still sending out weak signals every now and then.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1092`

## SensorReadings2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadings2&lang=en)

### 中文

他们找到了一座在轨的古老采矿站，似乎在很久以前就被其拥有者抛弃了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:996`

### English

They found an old mining station in orbit, seemingly abandoned by their owners a long time ago.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1093`

## SensorReadings3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadings3&lang=en)

### 中文

追逐微弱信号之旅带领着他们来到了一处空置的小型哨站，然而周边迹象显示某个技术水平和我们不相上下的存在新近在此地出现过。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:997`

### English

The hunt for the weak signal led them to a small empty outpost, yet there were signs that someone of a similar technological level to our own had been there fairly recently.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1094`

## SensorReadings4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadings4&lang=en)

### 中文

在信标地址周边满是散落的船壳和其他垃圾，经判断肯定是某时刻来自于一个一度存在过的大型恒星基地——然而没有任何迹象表明附近有这样一个结构存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:998`

### English

All across the signal beacon site were pieces of hull and other detritus which must have come from a larger starbase at one point - yet no other signs of such a structure remain.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1095`

## SensorReadingsSociety1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsSociety1&lang=en)

### 中文

当他们搜索地址周边区域的时候，设法找到了一个看上去像是空救生舱的东西，里面还有一个未知的、外星生命体的残骸。他们打算尽可能快地将样品带回做进一步测试。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1000`

### English

As they searched through the site, they managed to find a seemingly vacant lifepod with what looked like it the remains of an unknown, alien lifeform. They will bring the sample back for further tests as soon as they can.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1098`

## SensorReadingsSociety2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsSociety2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们之前捕捉到的信号读数来源于一台仍然以某种方式运转的机器，我们的小队设法从机器中提取到了一些有关制造它的文明的文件。由于我们先前对这个文明不甚了解，我们将其纳入了数据库——尽管他们看上去来自于一个更加古老的纪元。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1001`

### English

One of the machines responsible for the signals we had picked up on was still somewhat operable, and our team managed to extract some files about the civilization it supposedly hails from. As they are previously unknown to us they will be entered into our databases - though they seem to be from a much older era.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1099`

## SensorReadingsSociety3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsSociety3&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管当地了无感性生命迹象，此地仍然是很多动物的繁育之地，而且这些动物看上去学会了使用破损的机械作为求爱展示的道具。我们的小队将会带回其中一部分以作进一步的留观。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1002`

### English

Though uninhabited by sentient beings, it is the breeding grounds for a group of animals, who have seemingly learned to use some of the broken machinery as part of their courtship display. Our team will bring some of them back to be monitored further.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1100`

## SensorReadingsSociety3Gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsSociety3Gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

看上去当地的野生动物使用了部分内部机械作为它们求爱展示的道具。尽管我们通常对于这些动物毫无兴趣，我们的[root.GetIndividualNamePlural]还是将这些有趣生物的一部分带回作后续的留观。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1003`

### English

It seems the local wildlife have started using the machinery from the interior as part of their courtship display. Though usually of little interest to us, our [root.GetIndividualNamePlural] will bring some of these curious beings back to be monitored further.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1101`

## SensorReadingsSociety4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsSociety4&lang=en)

### 中文

当他们对当前区域进行筛选的时候，他们遇到了一座小型图书馆，里面满是记载了各种当地动物的生物学细节的书籍，这些书籍来自许多原定居者曾经造访过的行星。我们的小队将会带回这些书籍，并开始破解书中使用的语言，并且将其中提取到的一切信息添加到我们的数据库。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1004`

### English

As they sifted through the area, they came across a small library with books they think detail the biology of various local fauna, from the many different planets the original inhabitants had visited. Our team will bring them back and start deciphering the language used in the books, and add whatever information they can glean from them to our own databases.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1102`

## SensorReadingsSociety4Gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsSociety4Gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

在对该地点执行彻底搜寻之后，我们发现了一处图书馆。其中的书籍详尽地介绍了几千种外星植物的生物学细节，标本来自于原居住者造访过的每一个星球。我们会将这些书籍带回改善我们的数据库。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1005`

### English

After scouring the location, we discovered a small library. The books inside detailed the biology of thousands of exo-fauna, specimens from each of the planets the original inhabitants visited. These will be brought back to improve our own database.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1103`

## SensorReadingsPhysics1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsPhysics1&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管没有剩下什么有用的东西，我们的小队还是找到了一些记载不同行星的地质活动的研究论文——至少根据上面的图画，他们自己是这么认为的。他们会将自己认为能够抢救的一切资料带回，然后尽可能快地对其进行破解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1006`

### English

Though not much of use was left behind, our team managed to find some research papers on geological activity from different planets - or at least so they think, based on the illustrations. They will bring everything they think can survive the journey back, to be deciphered as soon as possible.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1105`

## SensorReadingsPhysics1Gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsPhysics1Gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管没有剩下什么有用的材料，我们的工程无人机还是定位到了一些记载不同行星的地质活动的研究论文。能够抢救的一切资料将会被带回，然后尽可能快地对其进行破解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1007`

### English

Though very few useful materials were left behind, our engineering drones did locate some research papers on geological activity from different planets. Everything that can survive the journey back will be returned, and deciphered as soon as possible.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1106`

## SensorReadingsPhysics2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsPhysics2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的小队花了大量的时间试图攻破那个尚在运转的机器的防御，结果发现它们里面什么有用的东西都没有。不过，机器本身激起了他们的好奇心，他们会带一些部件回来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1008`

### English

Our team spent a significant amount of time trying to crack the defenses on those machines which are still active, only to find they contained nothing of real interest. The machines themselves have piqued their interest though, and they will bring some parts back.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1107`

## SensorReadingsPhysics2Gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsPhysics2Gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们投入了非常多的时间攻破那个发送信号的机器，现在可以肯定它藏有什么秘密尚未重见天日。无论我们能够回收什么部件，都会在第一时间将其带回。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1009`

### English

After devoting a considerable amount of time to crack the defenses of the machines responsible for the signals, we now believe they may hold secrets yet to be divulged. Whatever parts we can salvage will be brought back right away.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1108`

## SensorReadingsPhysics3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsPhysics3&lang=en)

### 中文

他们发现一台可能制造了围绕该区域的薄弱屏障的机器——这可能是我们最初能找到这些东西的唯一原因。其他的东西一文不值，他们花了一些时间进行了拆解以期有所发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1010`

### English

They found a small machine which seems to be responsible for a weak barrier surrounding the area - probably the only reason there's anything to be found in the first place. With nothing else of value there, they have spent their time picking it apart, to see what they can learn from it.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1109`

## SensorReadingsPhysics4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsPhysics4&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管缺乏保养或维护，但似乎有一台此前用于支持此处参与设施的聚变反应堆仍在继续运行。我们的小队对此特别感兴趣，并将对其进行进一步调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1011`

### English

It seems a fusion reactor was keeping what was left of the place still running, in spite of a lack of care or maintenance. Our team have taken a special interest in it, and will investigate it further.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1110`

## SensorReadingsPhysics4Gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsPhysics4Gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管缺乏必要的维护，有一台此前用于支持此处参与设施的聚变反应堆仍在继续运行。我们的工程子个体将对其进行进一步调查，以期能有所发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1012`

### English

An old fusion reactor was keeping what was left of the place still running, in spite of the lack of necessary maintenance. Our engineering drones will investigate it further to see if there is anything we can learn from it.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1111`

## SensorReadingsEngineering1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsEngineering1&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们的专家在此处的废墟中梳理时，他们找到了一些几乎完好无损的舰船部件。虽然在长久的年岁中劣化失效，但我们的工程师相信他们可以从中筛选出一些信息

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1013`

### English

When they were combing through the ruins of the place, our specialists managed to find some mostly intact ship parts. Though no longer functional due to degradation over time, our engineers believe they can sift some information from them.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1113`

## SensorReadingsEngineering1Gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsEngineering1Gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

在梳理废墟时，我们的[root.GetIndividualNamePlural]发现了一些舰船部件。尽管由于长期劣化而不堪使用，但我们相信还是可以从中筛选出一些信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1014`

### English

As we combed the ruins, our [root.GetIndividualNamePlural] came upon several ship parts. Though no longer functional due to degradation over time, we believe we can sift some information from the pieces.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1114`

## SensorReadingsEngineering2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsEngineering2&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管此处颇为荒凉，我们的专家们还是发现了一些前所未见的建筑材料。他们完成发掘和分析工作之后，样品将会送回进行进一步测试。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1015`

### English

Though it is somewhat desolate, our specialists have found several uses of a building material they have never seen before. When they have finished their excavations and analysis of the area, samples will be brought back for further testing.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1115`

## SensorReadingsEngineering2Gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsEngineering2Gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[root.GetIndividualNamePlural]发现了一件我们此前从未见过的建筑材料。尽管应该无法直接使用，但我们可以将其带回去进行进一步分析。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1016`

### English

Our [root.GetIndividualNamePlural] have come upon a building material unlike any we have ever seen before. Though it is unlikely that it can be of use to us, it will be brought back to be analyzed further.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1116`

## SensorReadingsEngineering3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsEngineering3&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管没有在此处发现任何感性生物，我们的发掘人员还是在往深处挖掘时发现了一些无人机。它们已经严重受损，难以完整地进行逆向工程，但我们的技术人员相信这些分散的部件仍能给我们提供新的建造科技。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1017`

### English

Though there are no sentient beings to be found, our excavators found several drones when they dug a bit deeper. Though they are damaged beyond the point of being able to be reverse-engineered in their totality, our technicians believe their disparate parts might teach us new building techniques.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1117`

## SensorReadingsEngineering3Gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsEngineering3Gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的发掘[root.GetIndividualNamePlural]发现了一些与我们自身集体意识底层相似的信号，再稍微深挖之后发现了两台老旧损坏的自动机器人。尽管损伤已经无法修复，但我们相信通过对它们的部件进行逆向工程，我们还是能对它们的建造方式有更多了解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1018`

### English

Our excavation [root.GetIndividualNamePlural] came upon a signal similar to that which underlies our own shared consciousness, and after digging a bit deeper a pair of old broken automatons. Though damaged beyond repair, we believe we can reverse-engineer some of their parts to gain greater insight into how they once worked.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1118`

## SensorReadingsEngineering4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsEngineering4&lang=en)

### 中文

在废墟中筛选时，他们发现了一个曾存放着材料的小金库残骸，现在空无一物——很可能是很久以前就被海盗偷走的了。然而，其中还留下了一些关于原始居民如何开采这些相同材料的蓝图，这可能会派上用场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1019`

### English

As they were sifting through the ruins they found the remains of a small vault that once held materials, now empty - and most likely stolen by pirates, perhaps ages ago. They left behind some blueprints of how the original inhabitants had been mining these very same materials however, which might come in handy.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1119`

## SensorReadingsRare1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsRare1&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管此处一片狼藉，我们的团队还是找到了一台完全没有被时间、野生动物或海盗所损伤的机器。它们相信只要能够尽快破译它，其中一定蕴含着能带来科学突破的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1020`

### English

Despite the damage done to the rest of the location, our team managed to find a machine entirely unscathed by time, wildlife, or pirates. They believe it might hold the secrets of a scientific breakthrough, as soon as they can decipher them.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1121`

## SensorReadingsRare1Gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsRare1Gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管此处其他部分七零八落，我们的[root.GetIndividualNamePlural]还是发现了一台没有被时间、野生动物或海盗损坏的机器。只要能尽快破译它，我们就有希望能从中发掘出深埋的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1021`

### English

Though most of the location is falling apart, our [root.GetIndividualNamePlural] managed to find a machine entirely unscathed by time, wildlife, or pirates. We hope that it might divulge some long forgotten secrets - once we manage to decipher it.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1122`

## SensorReadingsRare2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsRare2&lang=en)

### 中文

就在我们的团队要离开这个区域时，他们发现了一小箱蓝图和研究论文。据我们的专家称，这些内容不但很容易破解，而且其中的一些甚至可以解决一些我们最近开展的研究中所遇到的难题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1022`

### English

As they were about to leave the area, our team came across a cabinet of blueprints and research papers. Not only were they supposedly easy to decipher, but our specialists believe some of them might shine a light on some research we had only recently begun working on ourselves.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1123`

## SensorReadingsRare2Gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASensorReadingsRare2Gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

就在我们的维护[root.GetIndividualNamePlural]要离开这个区域前，他们发现了一小箱蓝图和研究论文。这些内容不但很容易破解，而且我们相信其中的一些甚至可以解决一些我们最近开展的研究中所遇到的难题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1023`

### English

As we looked through the area one last time, one of our [root.GetIndividualNamePlural] came across a cabinet of blueprints and research papers. Not only were they easy to decipher, but we believe they might shine a light on some research we had only recently begun working on ourselves.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1124`

## specialist.1150.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspecialist.1150.title&lang=en)

### 中文

发现资源

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1025`

### English

Resource Discovery

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1127`

## specialist.1150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspecialist.1150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在搜索我们控制的星系时，我们的一架无人机从[prospectorium_discovery_target.System.GetName]获取了一些零散的读数。这个星系可能藏着什么我们意想不到的东西。\n\n尽管信号混杂在一起，但它们可能暗示了几种不同的来源，我们的无人机很难同时搜索所有这些信号源。我们希望能够调查：

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1026`

### English

While scouring the systems under our control, one of our drones picked up on a few scattered readings from [prospectorium_discovery_target.System.GetName]. There might be more to this system than we originally thought.\n\nMixed as the signals were, they could be suggesting several different resources, and it will be difficult for our drones to search for all of them at once. We would prefer if they would look into:

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1128`
