### 中文

[prospectorium_discovery_target.ResourceDiscovery]\n\n[prospectorium_discovery_target.ResourceDiscoveryLivingMetal]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1062`

### English

[prospectorium_discovery_target.ResourceDiscovery]\n\n[prospectorium_discovery_target.ResourceDiscoveryLivingMetal]

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1173`

## ResourceDiscovery1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscovery1&lang=en)

### 中文

就在[FromFromFrom.GetName]的地表之下我们发现了一处破旧的结构，因为时间的流逝被隐藏了起来。尽管它的结构某种程度上可以说完好无损，但是在建筑内部却没有什么值得回收的。不过，我们发现了一条通往地底更深处的通道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1064`

### English

Just beneath the surface of [FromFromFrom.GetName] we found an dilapidated structure, hidden away by the passage of time. Though its framework remains somewhat intact, there is nothing worth salvaging inside the building itself. However, we have discovered a passage delving further underground.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1176`

## ResourceDiscovery2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscovery2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[FromFromFrom.GetName]找到了一群不知所措的小机器，它们仍在尝试完成设定的任务。这些机器聚集在一个被碎片和拆解的设备阻碍了的地点。主集线器已经下线并且基本失效，但有一个信号有规律地出现，明确地指引我们向地下探索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1065`

### English

We found a group of smaller, bewildered machines at [FromFromFrom.GetName], still trying to execute their designated task. The machines were grouped together at a focal point blocked by debris and dismantled equipment. The main hub is offline and mostly useless, though a signal occurring at regular intervals and leading deeper underground has given us a clear direction to follow.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1177`

## ResourceDiscovery3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscovery3&lang=en)

### 中文

我们此前对[FromFromFrom.GetName]的分析发现了一座位于地表上的坑洞附近的基础研究中心，通往一个巨大的地下洞穴。研究中心和其中的机械已经无法挽救，我们会着眼搜寻洞穴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1066`

### English

Our early analysis of [FromFromFrom.GetName] uncovered a basic research center around a hole on the surface that led to a massive underground cavern. With the research center and its machinery entirely unsalvageable, we have refocused on surveying the cavern.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1178`

## ResourceDiscovery4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscovery4&lang=en)

### 中文

对[FromFromFrom.GetName]地表进行详尽的调查后，我们发现了一个正在对该区域进行高密度扫描的探测器。无论我们做什么如何尝试交互，这个探测器都只会返回这一个地点继续原来的动作。依照它的行为模式，我们开始对地下的区域进行扫描。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1067`

### English

After an exhaustive survey of the surface of [FromFromFrom.GetName] we discovered a probe that was engaged in an intensive scan of the region. No matter what we do or how we try to interfere, the probe will inevitably return to that one spot to continue its operations. Following its pattern of behavior, we have begun to survey the ground beneath.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1179`

## ResourceDiscovery5

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscovery5&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[FromFromFrom.GetName]发现了一座古老的采矿站或是研究站的废墟。似乎它是在过去的某一时候坠毁于此的。同时其上还有着受到攻击的迹象。尽管受到了严重的损伤，这座空间站仍然将信号向地下传输。在继续下一步之前，我们毫无疑问应该对此进行深究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1068`

### English

We have come across an old and ruined mining or research station at [FromFromFrom.GetName]. It seems likely that this crashed to the surface at some point in the past. It also shows signs of having been attacked. Despite the extensive damage, the station is continuing to direct a signal underground. Before moving on, we should no doubt investigate this further.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1180`

## ResourceDiscoveryEnergy1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryEnergy1&lang=en)

### 中文

它似乎与一个流动在地表下来源不明的电流相连。更重要的是，我们无法确定电流会持续多久，是否稳定，但我们相信我们可以利用它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1070`

### English

It seems to have been attached to an electric current running below the surface, the origin of which remains unclear.  What's more, we have no way to determine how long the current will last, or if it is entirely stable, but we believe we can make use of it.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1183`

## ResourceDiscoveryEnergy2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryEnergy2&lang=en)

### 中文

它似乎是为了维持一台古老的发电机运转而建造并隐藏在地表之下的。尽管存在很多设备问题，我们可以认定发电机仍运行良好。再费些功夫，我们应该就能够稳定它并为我们所用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1071`

### English

It appears to have been built to keep an archaic generator alive and hidden just below the surface, and despite all the equipment problems one might expect the generator appears to be running fine. With some work, we should be able to stabilize it and make use of it ourselves.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1184`

## ResourceDiscoveryEnergy3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryEnergy3&lang=en)

### 中文

当进一步观察神秘的发电机时，我们发现它连接在几条贯穿[FromFromFrom.GetName]的大电缆上。整个大家伙似乎都是由一个位于地表以下的单一设备运行的。只需对电流稍作修改，我们就可以利用它产生的能量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1072`

### English

When we looked further into the mysterious generator, we found it is attached to several large cables running throughout [FromFromFrom.GetName]. The whole thing seems to be run by a single apparatus housed far below the surface. With only a slight modification to the current, we can probably make use of the energy it creates.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1185`

## ResourceDiscoveryMinerals1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryMinerals1&lang=en)

### 中文

这台发电机连接到一处埋藏于底下的矿脉，其中可能蕴藏着对这台机器的运转者而言至关重要的原材料资源。尽管已经快要枯竭，我们仍可以将这些资源据为己有。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1074`

### English

The generator is attached to a mineral vein buried below ground, once probably an important source of raw material for whoever was in charge of the machine's operations. Though somewhat diminished, we can make use of these resources ourselves.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1188`

## ResourceDiscoveryMinerals2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryMinerals2&lang=en)

### 中文

进一步的探索发现这座发电机连接到了一处昏暗的、蕴藏着一些小型矿脉的人工洞穴。在地面上四处散布着一种此前未知的石质物种的尸体。目前还不清楚他们是在开采这些矿物，还是直接在地里开吃，但不管怎么样，他们身上密布的矿物突起对我们可能相当有使用价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1075`

### English

Further exploration revealed the generator is connected to a dim grotto complete with several smaller veins of minerals. Scattered about the floor were the bodies of a previously unknown lithoid species. It is unclear if they were harvesting the minerals or eating them straight from the ground, but in either case the bodies are encrusted with mineral protrusions that may well prove useful to us.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1189`

## ResourceDiscoveryMinerals3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryMinerals3&lang=en)

### 中文

这座发电机建立于一处巨大的地下洞穴中，填满了各种各样的矿物。很可能这整座巨大的洞穴就是被它自己给挖空的。它可能是出于对深入行星地壳的需求的结果而进行的挖掘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1076`

### English

The generator is built within a gigantic underground chamber, filled to the brim with minerals of all kinds. Emptying the grotto is going to be an endeavor in itself. Digging is likely to result in the need to push deeper into the world's crust.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1190`

## ResourceDiscoveryAlloys1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryAlloys1&lang=en)

### 中文

对这片区域的进一步探索发现，它连接到一处似乎还在保持一定运转的远古设施。利用现有资源，这座机器继续在往外吐出锻造好的崭新而好用的合金锭。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1078`

### English

Further exploration of the area revealed it is connected to an ancient apparatus somehow still continuing its operations. Using the resources on hand, the machine continues to spit out well-forged and entirely pristine bricks of useful alloys.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1193`

## ResourceDiscoveryAlloys2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryAlloys2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了一处奇怪的储存设施，其中塞满了支离破碎的舰船部件。尽管这些部件已经无可救药了，但我们可以把这些材料回收利用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1079`

### English

We have uncovered an odd storage facility filled with battered ship parts. Though the parts themselves are unsalvageable, we could possibly reuse some of the materials.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1194`

## ResourceDiscoveryAlloys3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryAlloys3&lang=en)

### 中文

对这个区域的进一步调查发现了一处深藏于地下的机库。在库房里我们发现了一些已经残破不堪的舰船，上面丝毫找不到它们前主人的线索。恢复机库里的这些舰船相当困难，但我们应该可以抢救回一些对我们仍有用的部件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1080`

### English

Extended surveys of the region led to the discovery of a hangar bay hidden deep below ground. Around the rooms we find several decayed ships, with no signs of whoever once owned them. Though recreating the ships that once stood in the hangar will be difficult, we should be able to salvage the parts for our own purposes.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1195`

## ResourceDiscoveryRareCrystals1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryRareCrystals1&lang=en)

### 中文

它看上去同一个采矿装置连接在一起，后者用于开采一种罕见的、古怪的晶体矿脉类型，尽管现场没有任何标识说明是谁在负责这个设施。在经过一些轻微的调整之后，我们能够将其为己所用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1082`

### English

It seems to have been connected to the mining operations of an unusual and oddly off-putting type of crystal growth, though there is no sign of whoever must have been in charge of this facility. With some minor adjustments, we should be able to make use of it ourselves.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1198`

## ResourceDiscoveryRareCrystals2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryRareCrystals2&lang=en)

### 中文

它引向了一个人工洞穴，里面满是精妙的晶体生物——可能这里是某种地下农场或者放牧地。由于很久以前就遭废弃，这些生物仅凭自身意愿随意蔓延生长。由于他们看上去很友好好客，如果我们有意愿，可以将这个农场恢复正常运转。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1083`

### English

It led to a grotto teeming with delicate crystalline creatures - perhaps some sort of underground farm or breeding grounds. Long abandoned, the creatures are now free to roam as they wish. As they seem friendly and welcoming, we should be able to restore the farm if we so wish.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1199`

## ResourceDiscoveryRareCrystals3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryRareCrystals3&lang=en)

### 中文

紧接着，一个摇摇欲坠的矿井出现在面前，里面满是无人机仍然在应付墙上的晶状体隆起物。它们完全没有因我们的出现受到影响，依旧不断地将大型集装箱填满并放到一边。尽管大多数晶体都是随处可见的类型，基本没什么价值，但是我们在中间找到了一些非常稀有的品种。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1084`

### English

Following it revealed a rickety mine shaft filled with drones still hacking away at crystalline protrusions on the walls. Undisturbed by our presence, they worked continually to fill and set aside large crates. Though many of the crystals are common varieties and largely worthless, there are some rare ones to be found among them.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1200`

## ResourceDiscoveryVolatileMotes1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryVolatileMotes1&lang=en)

### 中文

沿着路继续向前，我们撞见了某种球状两栖生物的巢穴，其附近的空气中有一种奇怪的非自然粒子在不停地震荡。我们尚且不清楚为什么这些粒子对这种生物有着如此的吸引力，但我们相信只要投入一丁点工作，就能将这些粒子稳定并利用起来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1086`

### English

Following it we come across the den of a bulbous amphibian creature, with vibrant, oddly unnatural particles vibrating through in the air around it. It is unclear why these particles are so attracted to the creature, but we believe that with a little work, we can stabilize and make use of them ourselves.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1203`

## ResourceDiscoveryVolatileMotes2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryVolatileMotes2&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们继续探险，我们在更深处发现了一处紧凑的设施，其中有几个房间，里面的机器被放在一边用于稳定易爆微粒。尽管目前是安全的，这些机器很快就会达到亟需修理的临界状态；我们应当集中我们的努力将其恢复正常，以免这些机器工作到散架。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1087`

### English

As we continued our explorations, we discovered a compact facility further below, with several rooms and machines set aside for stabilizing volatile motes. Though safe for now, they will soon reach a critical stage of disrepair, and we should focus our efforts on restoring them before they fall apart.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1204`

## ResourceDiscoveryVolatileMotes3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryVolatileMotes3&lang=en)

### 中文

这个房室引导我们前往地下更深处，我们最终走到了一个人工洞穴，里面满是有毒的废料，慢慢地侵蚀着地面。尽管充满危险，但似乎这里就是我们先前发现的易爆微粒的来源。只要我们小心地执行稳定化流程，我们应当可以对此地加以利用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1088`

### English

The chamber leading us further underground, we eventually came across a grotto filled with toxic waste, slowly eating away at the ground. Though dangerous, it seems to be the source of the volatile motes we discovered earlier. We should be able to make use of these, as long as we are careful with the stabilization process.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1205`

## ResourceDiscoveryExoticGases1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryExoticGases1&lang=en)

### 中文

由于某些原因，它似乎同某个埋在地下的废料处理站连接在了一起。这个站点十分古老，想要推测它的起源是不可能的事情，但我们相信可以收集一些这里的异星天然气为己所用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1090`

### English

For some reason, it seems to be connected to a waste disposal site deep below the ground. The site is so ancient that it will likely prove impossible to speculate on its origins, but we believe we can harvest some of these toxic gases for our own purposes.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1208`

## ResourceDiscoveryExoticGases2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryExoticGases2&lang=en)

### 中文

它看上去同一个拥挤的研究设施连接在了一起，这个设施位于一个有毒天然气的自然产生点，气体不断地从[FromFromFrom.GetName]的地底喷涌而出。对这一设施进行重新调整应该可以让我们收集这一些气体为己所用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1091`

### English

It seems to have been attached to a crowded research facility centered around a natural occurrence of toxic gases stemming from the core of [FromFromFrom.GetName]. Readjusting the facility somewhat should allow us to harvest these for ourselves.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1209`

## ResourceDiscoveryExoticGases3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryExoticGases3&lang=en)

### 中文

它引导着我们来到了远远深于地表之处，在那里我们遇到了一台规模大到难以置信的机器，很有可能是用于稳定[FromFromFrom.GetName]，但是现在已经损毁过重无法修复。尽管无法修整，这台处于损伤状态的机器能够产出某几种有毒气体，或许能对我们派上用场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1092`

### English

It led further below the surface where we came across a machine of incredible proportions, probably once used to stabilize [FromFromFrom.GetName], but now damaged beyond repair. Though unsalvageable, the machine's damaged state has led to the creation of several toxic gases that we might make use of.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1210`

## ResourceDiscoveryZro1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryZro1&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们跟上脚步，我们在地表的下面发现了泽珞的源头。来自外星文明的采集工具在房室的走廊上四散摆放，只要施加一点才智我们应该就能亲自使用这些工具。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1094`

### English

As we continued to follow it, we found a source of zro buried deep below the surface. Exotic harvesting tools littered the chamber floor, and with a little ingenuity we should be able to make use of them ourselves.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1213`

## ResourceDiscoveryZro2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryZro2&lang=en)

### 中文

它同一个埋藏在地表深处、已经不再运转的设施连接在一起。这个设施本身看上去似乎是打算调查一个泽珞的撞击点。尽管内部空空，绝大多数功能已经下线，将此地再次使用起来满足我们自己的目的应该是可行的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1095`

### English

It is connected to a defunct facility hidden in the depths below. The facility itself appears intended to survey a zro impact site. Though empty and largely offline, it should eventually be possible to reuse it for our own purposes.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1214`

## ResourceDiscoveryDarkMatter1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryDarkMatter1&lang=en)

### 中文

通过某种复杂的过程，它似乎同一个研究无人机连接在了一起。这个无人机环绕着一个深埋于行星地表之下的微小黑洞，可能是在研究这个黑洞的过程中不知通过什么方式稳定了它的奇点。尽管我们无法重现这项技术上的壮举，但我们可以充分利用这个无人机为己所用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1097`

### English

Through a complex process, it seems to be attached to a research drone. Circling a micro black hole located far below the planet's surface the drone has somehow managed to stabilize the singularity, perhaps in an effort to research it. Though we are unable to replicate this technological feat, we should be able to utilize the drone for our own means.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1217`

## ResourceDiscoveryLivingMetal1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryLivingMetal1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现它引向了一个远在地表之下的房室。在那里我们看到活体金属和某个我们认为过去是软体类的生物，以一种令人瞠目结舌的方式聚结在了一起。这两种事物已经融合在了一起，沿着地底下的洞穴网络慢慢地向外延伸。通过一定的努力，我们或许可以在不对这个生物造成太大伤害的前提下，小心地凿落一些金属的碎片。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1099`

### English

We have discovered that it leads to a chamber further below the surface. There we chanced upon a beguiling amalgamation of living metal and what we believe was once a molluscoid creature. It seems the two have fused together, slowly expanding through the cavern networks below the ground. With some effort, we should be able to start chipping pieces off the metal, for our own uses, without doing the creature too much harm.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1220`

## ResourceDiscoveryLivingMetal2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AResourceDiscoveryLivingMetal2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们没有花太多功夫，就发现活体金属集中在一个狭窄的人工房室中。由于它会滑动，并且对触碰能够产生反应，我们相信它的生命程度比我们一开始料想得要更高。如果我们对它小心地收集，我们能够在不杀死这个生物的前提下，最大程度地利用它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1100`

### English

It didn't take long for us to find the living metal is centered on a narrow grotto. As it slithers and reacts to touch, we believe it might be more alive than one might first think. If we harvest it carefully, we should be able to maximize our use of the creature, without killing it.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1221`

## prospectorium_resource_discovery_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprospectorium_resource_discovery_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们捕捉到了一些信号，表明在[prospectorium_discovery_target.System.GetName]可能存在更有价值的资源有待发现。我们已经发射了一些先验探针对这一区域进行遍寻——这样当我们能够派遣一艘舰船前往的时候，我们就能获得足够的数据进行深入研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1101`

### English

We have picked up on signals indicating there might be more valuable resources to be uncovered at [prospectorium_discovery_target.System.GetName]. We have sent some preliminary drones to scour the region - so it will be ready for further analysis and work when we can send a ship there.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1223`

## trade_action_share_empire_data_desc

### 中文

§L说实话，附庸没有向开明的宗主国隐藏任何秘密的道理。反之亦然，它们也不应当被完全蒙在鼓里！§!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1261`

## trade_action_bulwark_technology_desc

### 中文

§L不要将这样的行为视作我们在征用恒星基地科技。要这么看，你们是在放心地把控制权交给更加精明有学识的管理机构。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1132`

§LDon't think of it as us appropriating starbase technology. Think of it as entrusting control to more sagacious management.§!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1265`

## trade_action_scholarium_processors_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrade_action_scholarium_processors_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§L我们附庸有限的脑力应该专注在有限的事项上。下等的脑力负责收集数据，上级的宗主更适合权衡利弊。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1138`

### English

§LOur vassals' limited minds should be kept to limited options. Overlords are better at leveraging the data lesser minds gather.§!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1271`

## trade_action_prospectorium_techniques_desc

### 中文

§L我们知道，自己的附庸正在撸起袖子加油开采资源，就让远见卓识的宗主好好地对他们进行管理吧。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1140`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1273`

## trade_action_prospectorium_technology

## trade_action_prospectorium_technology_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrade_action_prospectorium_technology_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§L我们的附庸真是聪明的不要不要的！他们为了学习更好的采矿技术付出了如此大的牺牲。为了尊重他们不倦的努力，我们应当把这项技术充分利用起来。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1142`

### English

§LOur vassals are so clever! They sacrificed so much to learn better mining techniques. We are honored to benefit from their tireless labor.§!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1275`

## preset_bulwark_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_bulwark_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

戍邦是一种着重发展高超军事技巧的附属国特化类型，代价是削弱基础资源生产。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1244`

### English

A Bulwark is a Specialist Subject type focused on military prowess at the cost of basic resource production.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1380`

## preset_scholarium_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_scholarium_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

学邦是一种着重科技进步的附属国特化类型，代价是削弱军事能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1245`

### English

A Scholarium is a Specialist Subject type focused on technological progress at the cost of reduced military capacity.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1381`

## preset_prospectorium_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_prospectorium_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

勘探邦是一种着重资源生产的附属国特化类型，代价是削弱研究产出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1246`

### English

A Prospectorium is a Specialist Subject type focused on resource production at the cost of reduced research output.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1382`

## preset_tributary_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_tributary_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

朝贡国是一种有责任每月向宗主上交基础资源税的附属国类型。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1247`

### English

A Tributary is a Subject type obligated to pay their Overlord a basic resource tax on a monthly basis.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1383`

## preset_vassal_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_vassal_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

附庸是一种宗主国和附属国双方都可以自由商议臣服协议、具有多种可选项目的附属国类型。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1248`

### English

A Vassal is a Subject type with an open subject agreement and several available options for both the Subject and the Overlord.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1384`

## preset_protectorate_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_protectorate_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

保护国是一种科技上明显落后于其宗主国的附属国类型。他们会在研究宗主国已经研究过的科技上获得巨量加成。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1249`

### English

A Protectorate is a Subject type with significantly weaker technology than their Overlord. They will receive a boost towards all technology their Overlord already possesses.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1385`

## preset_subsidiary_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_subsidiary_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

子公司是一种寰宇企业宗主国特有的附属国类型，在附属国协议中规定有以月度上交基础资源税的责任。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1250`

### English

A Subsidiary is a Subject type with a Corporate Overlord, and they are obligated to pay a monthly basic resource tax as part of their subject agreement.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1386`

## preset_dominion_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_dominion_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

神域是一种失落帝国特有的附属国类型，有责任每月向其远古的宗主上交基础资源税。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1251`

### English

A Dominion is a Fallen Empire Subject type obligated to pay a monthly basic resource tax to their ancient Overlord.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1387`

## preset_satellite_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_satellite_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

卫星国是一种失落帝国特有的附属国类型，有责任每月向其远古的宗主上交研究点税。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1252`

### English

A Satellite is a Fallen Empire Subject type obligated to pay a significant research tax to their ancient Overlord.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1388`

## preset_signatory_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_signatory_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

条约国是一种失落帝国特有的附属国类型，他们的外交完全受宗主国控制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1253`

### English

A Signatory is a Fallen Empire Subject type with no independent diplomacy from their ancient Overlord.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1389`

## preset_thrall_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_thrall_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

仆从国是一种失落帝国特有的附属国类型，有责任每月向其远古的宗主上交基础资源税，并且无权拓展边界。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1254`

### English

A Thrall is a Fallen Empire Subject type obligated to pay a monthly basic resource tax to their ancient Overlord and with no ability to expand their borders.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1390`

## preset_political_client_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_political_client_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

傀儡国是一种失落帝国特有的附属国类型，必须支持其远古宗主的所有政治事务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1255`

### English

A Political Client is a Fallen Empire Subject type that has to support their ancient Overlord in all matters political.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1391`

## preset_herald_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_herald_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

马前卒是一种失落帝国特有的附属国类型，有责任加入其远古宗主发起的所有战争。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1256`

### English

A Herald is a Fallen Empire Subject type obligated to join in every war their ancient Overlord starts.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1392`

## preset_scion_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_scion_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

先辈子弟是一种特殊的附属国类型，有一个失落帝国宗主知道他们的发展历程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1257`

### English

A Scion is a Subject type that was guided through their development by a Fallen Empire Overlord.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1393`

## preset_satrapy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_satrapy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

辖地是一种可汗特有的附属国类型，有责任每月向其战狂宗主上交舰队和基础资源税。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1258`

### English

A Satrapy is a Khan Subject type obligated to pay their warmongering Overlord both ships and basic resources.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1394`

## preset_relic_satrapy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_relic_satrapy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

辖地是一种延续了汗国传统的附属国类型，有责任每月向其宗主提供舰船和资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1259`

### English

A Satrapy is a Subject type continuing the traditions of a Khan that is obligated to provide their Overlord with both ships and resources.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1395`

## preset_bulwark_flavor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_bulwark_flavor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个附属国专精军事事务。他们因为在防务方面的出色表现而远近闻名，能够为宗主提供军事上的见解和防护。作为代价，他们单一的专注使得自身高度依赖外部物质的支援来保证国家的运转。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1261`

### English

This subject specializes in military affairs. They are known for their excellence in defensive matters, providing insight and protection for their overlord. In turn, their single-minded focus makes them highly reliant on material support to keep their empire running.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1398`

## preset_scholarium_flavor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_scholarium_flavor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个附属国专注科学与研究。由于几乎全部的预算都被投入到学术界和研究所中，他们非常需要宗主国提供保护，后者则能接收难以置信的先进科技，用来解决手头的麻烦。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1262`

### English

This subject specializes in research and science. With almost their entire budget focused on supporting their academics and researchers, they are in dire need of protection from their overlord, who receives incredible technology for their troubles.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1399`

## preset_prospectorium_flavor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_prospectorium_flavor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个附属国专精物质和制造。他们热情洋溢的心智使得他们能够在某些区域采掘到价值连城的矿物，而其他人对这些区域则望而却步。他们会充分发掘目光所及之处的每一颗小行星和卫星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1263`

### English

This subject specializes in materials and manufacturing. Their keen senses allow them to dig up the most valuable minerals from regions others have already given up on, making the most out of every asteroid or moon out there.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1400`

## preset_tributary_flavor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_tributary_flavor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

朝贡国是一种享有相当的独立自治权的附属国，能够自行负责自己的民生和政治事务。他们需要上缴大量的贡金以换取宗主国的保护，只要钱能源源不断地涌入宗主国的国库，那么基本上他们想做什么就可以做什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1264`

### English

A tributary is a fairly autonomous subject, still in charge of their own affairs both internally and politically. They pay a considerable amount of tribute for their overlord's protection, and as long as the money is flowing, they are mostly free to do whatever they want.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1401`

## preset_vassal_flavor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_vassal_flavor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

附庸是另一个帝国的附属国，基本上没有自治权或仅有零星的自治权。由于和外国势力签订了条约，他们的存活与否基本仰仗宗主国仁慈抑或严酷，并且必须屈服于宗主国凭借意愿提出的任何规则和条例。对于一些弱小的国家来说，他们离完全并入宗主国的掌控只差一步之遥，而这正是他们签下一纸纸丧权辱国条约的末路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1265`

### English

A vassal is a subject of another empire, often with little to no self-rule. Having signed a pact with a foreign power, they are often at the whims and mercy of their overlord, forced to bend to whatever rules and regulations their liege wishes. For some weakened empires, this is the final stopgap before they are consolidated properly into the fold, as they are signing away their autonomy bit by bit.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1402`

## preset_protectorate_flavor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_protectorate_flavor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

保护国是实力远远弱于宗主国的附属国，他们的攀附甚至说得上是国运亨通了。他们的领主会照看他们，抵挡来自星系深处的种种威胁，在此期间他们可以稳定和改善自己的家园。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1266`

### English

A protectorate is a subject that is considerably weaker than their overlord, reduced to the point where their vassalage is considered a boon to them. Protected from the dangers of the greater galaxy, they can stabilize and improve their homeland as their liege looks out for them.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1403`

## preset_subsidiary_flavor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_subsidiary_flavor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

子公司是拥有相当自治权的附属国，能够自由地管理自己的事务。他们通过合同交出了部分独立性，但合同的双方都期待双边的支持和援助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1267`

### English

A subsidiary is a subject with a considerable amount of self-rule, free to attend to their own affairs. They have signed away some of their independence for a deal where both sides are expected to support and aid one another.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1404`

## preset_dominion_flavor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_dominion_flavor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

神域是远古觉醒帝国的附属国。古老而强大的战舰越过他们的边境，他们将自己的主权呈递给了一个极度狂热的宗主来统治，后者要求他们彻底地臣服，无论是名义上还是精神上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1268`

### English

A dominion serves as a subject to an ancient awakened empire. With old yet powerful warships crossing their borders, they have subjected themselves to the rule of an overly zealous overlord who demands their subjugation in name and spirit.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1405`

## preset_satellite_flavor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_satellite_flavor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

卫星国是远古觉醒帝国的附属国。他们签署条约，将自治的权力出卖给了外国势力，因此领主会不断地对其实施管制，其文化软实力——无论是科技、艺术，或是其他的表达形式——都会受到上面仔细的监管。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1269`

### English

A satellite serves as a subject to an ancient awakened empire. Having signed away their self-rule to a foreign power, they are constantly governed by their liege: every little piece of cultural significance - whether that's technology, the arts, or another form of expression - is carefully regulated from afar.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1406`

## preset_signatory_flavor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_signatory_flavor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

条约国是远古觉醒帝国的附属国。尽管要受到上面的管辖，他们在很多方面被放手自行管理事务。由于他们不需要上交朝贡，也没有太多针对他们的规定，通常他们对于领主想要什么也不甚明晰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1270`

### English

A signatory serves as a subject to an ancient awakened empire. Though ruled from afar, they are in many ways left alone to attend to their own matters. Because they are not subjected to tribute or too many regulations, they are often unclear what the goals of their liege might be.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1407`

## preset_thrall_flavor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_thrall_flavor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

仆从国是远古觉醒帝国的附属国。他们要受到上面的严苛管辖，上缴数量相当的财富给雄心勃勃的宗主，后者会使用附庸的财富来喂饱自己的战争机器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1271`

### English

A thrall serves as a subject to an ancient awakened empire. Subjected to a harsh rule from afar, they pay a considerable amount of wealth to their ambitious overlord, who uses their vassals' wealth to fuel their own war machines.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1408`

## preset_scion_flavor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_scion_flavor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

先辈子弟是远古觉醒帝国的附属国。他们长期收到某位博爱的施恩者帝国的支持，因此在统治的方方面面都享有自治。他们偶尔还会收到领主的恩礼，至于理由，整个星系都摸不着头脑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1272`

### English

A scion serves as a subject to an ancient awakened empire. Long supported by an ancient philanthropic benefactor, they are autonomous in almost all aspects of their rule. They occasionally receive gifts or aid from their liege for reasons unfathomable to the rest of the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1409`

## preset_satrapy_flavor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_satrapy_flavor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

辖地是效力于大可汗的附属国。他们选择屈服于掠夺者汗国的军力，被赋予以大可汗的名义统治星系某片区域的权力。只要他们持续不断地上交舰船和资源以支持大可汗的征战，大可汗就不会过问他们自己的事务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1273`

### English

A satrapy serves as a subject to the Great Khan. Having bent to the military might of the marauders, they have been given the right to govern their region of the galaxy in the Great Khan's name. As long as they continue to support the marauders' ongoing conquests with ships and resources, they are left to attend to their own matters.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1410`

## preset_relic_satrapy_flavor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_relic_satrapy_flavor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

辖地是大可汗偏爱的附属国类型，如今王座易主，抛弃这一传统未免糟蹋。作为持续不断地上交舰船和资源以支持宗主国征战的回报，宗主国不会过问他们自己的事务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1274`

### English

A satrapy was the subject style favored by the Great Khan, now with a new leader on the throne it would be a waste to toss away such tradition. In return for supporting their Overlord's ongoing conquests with ships and resources, they are left to attend to their own matters.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1411`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1289`

### English

## pledged_allegiance_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apledged_allegiance_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在的宗主点燃了战争之火，为的是将我们从先前的主人手中解救出来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1293`

### English

Our current Overlord braved the fires of war to free us from our former masters.

## failed_betrayal_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afailed_betrayal_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们没能挣脱宗主国的掌握。我们别无选择，只能继续侍奉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1295`

### English

Our attempt to escape the grasp of our Overlord has failed. We have no choice but to serve.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1435`

## recent_failed_betrayal_overlord

## oppressive_terms_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aoppressive_terms_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些拜于我们脚下的蝼蚁唯一存在的价值是乖乖闭嘴，然后交上他们可悲的贡品。\n\n§E初始附属国协议条款\n$t$$subject_can_be_integrated$\n$t$$subject_can_not_do_diplomacy$\n$t$$subject_cannot_expand$\n$t$加入宗主国战争：所有\n$t$加入附属国战争：防御性\n$t$附属国地产限制：1§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1309`

### English

Those we subjugate are inferior to us, we tolerate them only because they remain silent and shower us with their pathetic gifts.\n\n§EInitial Vassal Agreement Terms:\n$t$$subject_can_be_integrated$\n$t$$subject_can_not_do_diplomacy$\n$t$$subject_cannot_expand$\n$t$Joins Overlord Wars: All\n$t$Joins Subject Wars: Defensive\n$t$Subject Holdings Limit: 1§!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1450`

## balanced_terms_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abalanced_terms_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们相信，只要能公平地对待我们的附属国。假以时日他们会幡然悔悟，明白我们的道路的智慧。\n\n§E初始附属国协议条款\n$t$$subject_can_not_be_integrated$\n$t$$subject_can_do_diplomacy_but_not_vote$\n$t$$subject_can_expand_with_tithe$\n$t$加入宗主国战争：防御性\n$t$加入附属国战争：防御性\n$t$附属国地产限制：1§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1311`

### English

We believe in treating our subjects fairly. Perhaps in time, they will see the wisdom of our ways and the errors of theirs.\n\n§EInitial Vassal Agreement Terms:\n$t$$subject_can_not_be_integrated$\n$t$$subject_can_do_diplomacy_but_not_vote$\n$t$$subject_can_expand_with_tithe$\n$t$Joins Overlord Wars: Defensive\n$t$Joins Subject Wars: Defensive\n$t$Subject Holdings Limit: 1§!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1452`

## benevolent_terms_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abenevolent_terms_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的附属国与我们是唇亡齿寒的关系，只要他们成功我们也算赢，所以我们要齐头并进。\n\n§E初始附属国协议条款\n$t$$subject_can_not_be_integrated$\n$t$$subject_can_do_diplomacy$\n$t$$subject_can_expand$\n$t$加入宗主国战争：防御性\n$t$加入附属国战争：防御性\n$t$附属国地产限制：1§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1313`

### English

Our subjects are as much part of us as we are of them, their success is our victory, and we shall move forward as one.\n\n§EInitial Vassal Agreement Terms:\n$t$$subject_can_not_be_integrated$\n$t$$subject_can_do_diplomacy$\n$t$$subject_can_expand$\n$t$Joins Overlord Wars: Defensive\n$t$Joins Subject Wars: Defensive\n$t$Subject Holdings Limit: 1§!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1454`

## oppressive_corporate_terms_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aoppressive_corporate_terms_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

他们对该收购的卑微抵抗是徒劳的；再坚定的信仰最终都会被金钱所打败。\n\n§E初始附属公司协议：\n$t$$subject_can_not_be_integrated$\n$t$$subject_can_do_diplomacy_but_not_vote$\n$t$$subject_can_expand_with_tithe$\n$t$基础资源税：45%\n$t$高级资源税：15%\n$t$加入宗主国战争：全部\n$t$加入附属国战争：防御\n$t$附属国地产限制：1§!\n\n这是一项§Y压迫型归顺政策§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1316`

### English

The pathetic attempt to block this acquisition has failed; no matter their convictions, in the end, everyone has a price.\n\n§EInitial Subsidiary Agreement Terms:\n$t$$subject_can_not_be_integrated$\n$t$$subject_can_do_diplomacy_but_not_vote$\n$t$$subject_can_expand_with_tithe$\n$t$Basic Resource Taxes: 45%\n$t$Advanced Resource Taxes: 15%\n$t$Joins Overlord Wars: All\n$t$Joins Subject Wars: Defensive\n$t$Subject Holdings Limit: 1§!\n\nThis is an §YOppressive Subjugation Policy§!.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1458`

## balanced_corporate_terms_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abalanced_corporate_terms_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

只要有令人满意的协定，我们双方都可以继续去好好赚钱。\n\n§E初始附属公司协议：\n$t$$subject_can_not_be_integrated$\n$t$$subject_can_do_diplomacy_but_not_vote$\n$t$$subject_can_expand_with_tithe$\n$t$基础资源税：30%\n$t$加入宗主国战争：防御\n$t$加入附属国战争：防御\n$t$附属国地产限制：1§!\n\n这是一项§Y平衡型归顺政策§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1319`

### English

With a satisfactory deal in place, both parties can now get back to work.\n\n§EInitial Subsidiary Agreement Terms:\n$t$$subject_can_not_be_integrated$\n$t$$subject_can_do_diplomacy_but_not_vote$\n$t$$subject_can_expand_with_tithe$\n$t$Basic Resource Taxes: 30%\n$t$Joins Overlord Wars: Defensive\n$t$Joins Subject Wars: Defensive\n$t$Subject Holdings Limit: 1§!\n\nThis is a §YBalanced Subjugation Policy§!.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1461`

## benevolent_corporate_terms_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abenevolent_corporate_terms_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

双赢才是好买卖；和生命一样，和谐才能使生意兴隆。\n\n§E初始附属公司协议：\n$t$$subject_can_not_be_integrated$\n$t$$subject_can_do_diplomacy$\n$t$$subject_can_expand$\n$t$基础资源税：30%\n$t$加入宗主国战争：防御\n$t$加入附属国战争：防御\n$t$附属国地产限制：1§!\n\n这是一项§Y施恩型归顺政策§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_subjects_l_simp_chinese.yml:1322`

### English

The best deals benefit both parties; like life itself, business flourishes in harmony.\n\n§EInitial Subsidiary Agreement Terms:\n$t$$subject_can_not_be_integrated$\n$t$$subject_can_do_diplomacy$\n$t$$subject_can_expand$\n$t$Basic Resource Taxes: 30%\n$t$Joins Overlord Wars: Defensive\n$t$Joins Subject Wars: Defensive\n$t$Subject Holdings Limit: 1§!\n\nThis is a §YBenevolent Subjugation Policy§!.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_subjects_l_english.yml:1464`
